    27 export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
    28   const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
    29   const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
    30   const [staffUser, setStaffUser] = useState<StaffUser | null>(null);
    31   const [organization, setOrganization] = useState<Organization | null>(null);
    32   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    33   const [mfaVerified, setMfaVerified] = useState(false);
    34   const [mfaStatus, setMfaStatus] = useState<MfaStatus>('loading');
    35 
    36   const checkMfaLevel = useCallback(async () => {
    37     try {
    38       const { data: aalData } = await supabase.auth.mfa.getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel();
    39       if (!aalData) { setMfaVerified(false); setMfaStatus('loading'); return; }
    40       // AAL2 means MFA challenge was passed this session
    41       const verified = aalData.currentLevel === 'aal2';
    42       setMfaVerified(verified);
    43       // nextLevel === 'aal2' means at least one factor is enrolled
    44       const enrolled = aalData.nextLevel === 'aal2' || aalData.currentLevel === 'aal2';
    45       setMfaStatus(enrolled ? 'enrolled' : 'not_enrolled');
    46     } catch {
    47       setMfaVerified(false);
    48       setMfaStatus('not_enrolled');
    49     }
    50   }, []);
    51 
    52   const fetchStaffUser = useCallback(async (userId: string) => {
    53     const { data, error } = await supabase
    54       .from('staff_users')
    55       .select('*')
    56       .eq('id', userId)
    57       .maybeSingle();
    58     if (!error && data) {
    59       setStaffUser(data as StaffUser);
    60       const { data: org } = await supabase
    61         .from('organizations')
    62         .select('*')
    63         .eq('id', data.organization_id)
    64         .maybeSingle();
    65       if (org) setOrganization(org as Organization);
    66     }
    67   }, []);
    68 
    69   useEffect(() => {
    70     const timeout = setTimeout(() => setLoading(false), 5000);
    71 
    72     supabase.auth.getSession().then(async ({ data: { session } }) => {
    73       clearTimeout(timeout);
    74       setSession(session);
    75       setUser(session?.user ?? null);
    76       if (session?.user) {
    77         await Promise.all([fetchStaffUser(session.user.id), checkMfaLevel()]);
    78         setLoading(false);
    79       } else {
    80         setLoading(false);
    81       }
    82     }).catch(() => {
    83       clearTimeout(timeout);
    84       setLoading(false);
    85     });
    86 
    87     const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(async (_event, session) => {
    88       setSession(session);
    89       setUser(session?.user ?? null);
    90       if (session?.user) {
    91         fetchStaffUser(session.user.id);
    92         checkMfaLevel();
    93       } else {
    94         setStaffUser(null);
    95         setOrganization(null);
    96         setMfaVerified(false);
    97         setMfaStatus('loading');
    98       }
    99     });
   100 
   101     return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
   102   }, [fetchStaffUser, checkMfaLevel]);
   103 
   104   const signIn = async (email: string, password: string) => {
   105     const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
   106     if (error) return { error: error.message };
   107     return { error: null };
   108   };
   109 
   110   const signOut = async () => {
   111     await supabase.auth.signOut();
   112     setStaffUser(null);
   113     setOrganization(null);
   114     setMfaVerified(false);
   115     setMfaStatus('loading');
   116   };
   117 
   118   const refreshStaffUser = async () => {
   119     if (user) await fetchStaffUser(user.id);
   120   };
   121 
   122   const refreshMfaStatus = async () => {
   123     await checkMfaLevel();
   124     if (user) await fetchStaffUser(user.id);
   125   };
   126 
   127   return (
   128     <AuthContext.Provider value={{
   129       user, session, staffUser, organization, loading,
   130       mfaVerified, mfaStatus,
   131       signIn, signOut, refreshStaffUser, refreshMfaStatus,
   132     }}>
   133       {children}
   134     </AuthContext.Provider>
   135   );
   136 }
   137 
   138 export function useAuth() {
   139   const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
   140   if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
   141   return ctx;
   142 }
   143

SkillObservation(content='Base directory for this skill: /workspace/app-cr8gr9rfgsn5/.skills/supabase-server\n\n# Supabase Backend\n\nApplies to all frontend stacks (Web / MiniProgram / App). Covers Supabase server-side Schema, RLS, Edge Functions, Storage, Realtime, and Auth configuration.\n\n- For **WeChat Mini-Program/Weapp**, read `references/miniprogram.md` (weapp-specific backend rules: persistence, anon RLS guard, remote-asset transfer, QR code generation).\n\n**Timezone**: Supabase runs in **UTC** by default. All `timestamptz` values are stored in UTC, `now()` returns UTC, and `pg_cron` schedules are interpreted in UTC. Convert to the user\'s local timezone in the frontend or via `AT TIME ZONE` in SQL when display/scheduling requires a specific offset.\n\n## Initial Setup\n\n- Use `supabase_init` to initialize the database or start it from a paused state\n\n## Schema Design\n\n- Minimize tables and columns; merge functionalities into single tables when possible\n- Set database-level default values for required fields\n  - Example: `owner_id uuid not null default auth.uid()`\n  - Example: `order_number text UNIQUE NOT NULL` — use a trigger bound to insert logic to auto-generate the order number\n- For multi-table relations, define them clearly to avoid missing link fields\n- ENUM values require explicit casts\n- **NEVER interact with `supabase/migrations/` directory** (including ls, cat, view)\n- Always apply changes through incremental migrations\n- Verify database state by querying table structure using `supabase_execute_sql`\n- Try to combine tasks and execute all SQL in a single run\n- Always consider performance and speed optimization\n- Disallow SQL statements with silent or ambiguous success semantics (e.g., `IF NOT EXISTS`, `IF EXISTS`) — all operations must fail explicitly when schema or data deviates from expectations\n\n## Data Insertion\n\n**Must-Add:**\n- System-required data (e.g., categories, form dropdown options)\n- **For image URLs**: Use `image_search` to get real URLs — **NEVER** use placeholder URLs like `https://placeholder-image.jpg`\n- Add initial seed data (INSERT INTO) in the migration SQL, using `image_search` for real image URLs\n\n**Allowed:**\n- Homepage display data (e.g., showcases, course previews, products)\n- Demo data (e.g., sample exam sets)\n\n**Forbidden:**\n- User data\n- User activity history (e.g., lottery, orders)\n- Real personal names or any privacy-related info\n\n## SQL Modification\n\n- If you have full context in memory, prioritize using it directly\n- If unsure about current data structure, query it first; do not rely on migration files\n- Under no circumstances should you view or modify migration files\n- If frontend requires a feature without a corresponding table, policy, or edge function, you may add it afterward\n- When modifying business data (non-DDL, policy, RPC, etc.), use `supabase_execute_sql`\n- Before writing api.ts, read the corresponding `supabase/migrations/*.sql` to confirm NOT NULL constraints for every column — context compression may lose field details; migrations are the ground truth\n\n## Security Policies (RLS)\n\n### Design Principles\n\n- **Every table MUST have RLS enabled** — no exceptions, regardless of whether login auth exists\n- **Full coverage per table**: Explicitly define policies for every combination of SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE × {anon, authenticated, admin}. **Never leave any blank** — missing policies silently return empty data or 0 affected rows\n  - Per-table review checklist: anon × {S/I/U/D}, authenticated × {S/I/U/D}, admin × {S/I/U/D}\n  - Anticipate future features: add corresponding constraints now for role×operation combinations that may be needed later\n- **INSERT always requires a matching SELECT**: Any role granted INSERT permission MUST also have a SELECT policy defined\n  - Example: If a policy "allows anyone to submit", add one so "submitters can view their own submissions"\n- Frontend and backend permissions must be aligned\n  - No permission, no interaction\n  - All submit-capable forms must have explicit, role-based permissions\n- PostgREST silently returns 0 affected rows (no error) when UPDATE/DELETE does not match RLS — writes appear to succeed but nothing changed. Tables the frontend needs to mutate must have explicit UPDATE/DELETE policies defined\n- Clear role boundaries\n  - Admins must have full read/write access\n  - Roles must have complete and secure permissions defined for every possible interaction scenario\n- All internal checks must use `SECURITY DEFINER`\n\n### Implementation Notes\n\n- RLS should only depend on **static columns or secure functions**\n- **Policy self-loop**: A policy\'s USING clause must not query the same table it protects — use a `SECURITY DEFINER` helper function instead (it bypasses RLS when querying the table)\n- Use public views to expose read-only access to selected fields of high-privilege tables\n- **The column checked by `auth.uid()` MUST auto-fill**: For each INSERT policy whose WITH CHECK contains `<col> = auth.uid()`, that exact `<col>` MUST be declared `NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid()`. The client never sends it. Apply this to that one column only — never blanket-default other uuid/FK columns (e.g. `assigned_to`), which would be a bug.\n  - ❌ `owner_id uuid NOT NULL` + client `.insert(payload)` → owner is NULL → "new row violates row-level security policy"\n  - ✅ `owner_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid()`\n\n## Storage Buckets\n\n- Create for image/file uploads only\n- **CRITICAL**: NEVER store images/videos as Base64 in database\n- ALL image/video assets MUST be uploaded to Supabase Storage\n- When third-party APIs return image/video URLs, transfer to Storage in Edge Function\n- buckets must be created in migration SQL via `supabase_apply_migration` — **do not** create them dynamically inside Edge Functions\n- Frontend validation: 1MB limit, snake_case filenames\n\n\n## Edge Functions\n\n### When to Use\n\n- **MANDATORY for third-party API calls**: ALL external API integrations (payment, weather, maps, etc.) MUST be implemented through Edge Functions\n- **STRICTLY FORBIDDEN**: Direct third-party API calls from client-side code (fetch/axios in frontend components)\n- Server-side secrets and service-role operations\n- Sensitive payment logic requiring secure environment\n\n### Authoring\n\n- Create files under `supabase/functions/<function_name>/`\n- **CRITICAL**: Use `supabase_deploy_edge_function` to deploy after modifying or creating Edge Function files\n- Put cross-domain content in the `_shared/` directory\n- No URL imports — only npm and JSR\n- Do not add secrets unless explicitly required by API documentation\n- Handle CORS properly\n- `supabase.sql` forbidden in Edge Functions\n- `supabase.raw` forbidden in Edge Functions — use `.rpc()` for atomic operations\n- Response Content-Type must be chosen only from: `[\'application/json\', \'application/octet-stream\', \'text/event-stream\', \'multipart/form-data\']`\n- Make proper use of optimistic locking to ensure that subsequent settlement operations in the state flow are executed only once, even if called multiple times\n\n\n## Supabase Auth\n\n- Your knowledge may be outdated. **MUST** call `skill_action(skill="login")` to refresh with the latest information\n- Any form of third-party login (OAuth or SSO) must use the login skill as the knowledge basis\n\n## Scheduled Tasks\n\nWhenever the app needs work to run **on a time schedule** or **automatically after a condition is met** (with no user action driving it), use a scheduled task. Typical cases:\n\n- **Scheduled notifications** — daily reminders, expiry/renewal alerts, fixed-time push\n- **Scheduled refresh** — refresh cached aggregates, sync external data, recompute rankings periodically\n- **Scheduled data analysis** — nightly report generation, periodic statistics roll-ups\n- **Conditional triggers** — "after condition X, do Y", e.g. *when traffic > 100, send a notification*; *when stock < threshold, alert*; *when an order is unpaid for 30 min, cancel it*\n\n**All of the above MUST use the official `pg_cron` pattern** — a `pg_cron` job periodically runs a database function that scans for due/matching rows and updates their state; for third-party side effects (push, email, external APIs) the job invokes an Edge Function via `pg_net`. The database only queries and updates state; notification/third-party logic stays in the Edge Function. Do **NOT** drive such work from frontend timers or rely on a user opening a page.\n\n**Timezone**: `pg_cron` schedules run in **UTC**. Compute the UTC cron expression from the intended local time (e.g. 9:00 in UTC+8 → `0 1 * * *`).\n\nWhen building any scheduled task, read `references/scheduled-tasks.md`.\n\n## Supabase Realtime (low-frequency feature)\n\nWhen realtime capability is genuinely needed, read `references/realtime.md` — it covers server-side publication switches, per-stack client subscription patterns, and RN/weapp limitations.\n\n**Multi-identity scenarios**: Identity selection / role assignment logic **must NOT** be placed on the frontend — it **MUST** be encapsulated in an Edge Function.', observation=<ObservationType.SKILL: 'skill'>, skill='supabase-server', success=True, data={'object_storage_path': 'skill-aws-online:aws-prod/miaoda_skills/prompts/system/supabase-server/'})

}

main().catch(console.error);

const { createClient } = require('@supabase/supabase-js');

const url = 'https://hgzyypyqawcppivnghpr.supabase.co';
const key = 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6Imhnenl5cHlxYXdjcHBpdm5naHByIiwicm9sZSI6InNlcnZpY2Vfcm9sZSIsImlhdCI6MTc4MzA0NDE3MCwiZXhwIjoyMDk4NjIwMTcwfQ.U8lrNfsqklbAzYa0jmjo3wWPpWmFazWDeRY47kGhYBs';

const supabase = createClient(url, key);

async function main() {
  console.log('=== staff_users ===');
  const { data: staff, error: staffErr } = await supabase.from('staff_users').select('id, full_name, role, organization_id, created_at, is_active');
  if (staffErr) console.error(staffErr); else console.table(staff);

  console.log('=== mailboxes ===');
  const { data: mailboxes, error: mbErr } = await supabase.from('mailboxes').select('id, local_part, display_name, status, created_at');
  if (mbErr) console.error(mbErr); else console.table(mailboxes);

  console.log('=== auth.users ===');
  const { data: authUsers, error: authErr } = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers();
  if (authErr) console.error(authErr); else console.table(authUsers.users.map(u => ({ id: u.id, email: u.email, created_at: u.created_at })));
}

main().catch(console.error);

cat > /workspace/app-cr8gr9rfgsn5/tasks/delete-demo-data.cjs << 'EOF'
const { createClient } = require('@supabase/supabase-js');

const url = 'https://hgzyypyqawcppivnghpr.supabase.co';
const key = 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6Imhnenl5cHlxYXdjcHBpdm5naHByIiwicm9sZSI6InNlcnZpY2Vfcm9sZSIsImlhdCI6MTc4MzA0NDE3MCwiZXhwIjoyMDk4NjIwMTcwfQ.U8lrNfsqklbAzYa0jmjo3wWPpWmFazWDeRY47kGhYBs';

const supabase = createClient(url, key);

const deleted = {
  contacts: [],
  mailbox: null,
  staffUser: null,
  authUser: null,
  signatures: [],
};

async function main() {
  // 1. Delete demo contacts (created by seed, external companies)
  const { data: contacts, error: contactsErr } = await supabase.from('contacts').select('id, email, name').in('email', [
    'logistics@totalenergies.com',
    'contracts@goil.com.gh',
    'invoicing@shell.com.gh',
    'hr@frimpsoil.com.gh',
    'corporate@gcbbank.com.gh',
    'info@kwesifillingstation.com',
    'hse@energycom.gov.gh',
    'e.tetteh@frimpsoil.com.gh'
  ]);
  if (contactsErr) throw contactsErr;

  if (contacts && contacts.length > 0) {
    const ids = contacts.map(c => c.id);
    const { error } = await supabase.from('contacts').delete().in('id', ids);
    if (error) throw error;
    deleted.contacts = contacts;
  }

  // 2. Delete demo mailbox (Kofi Mensah seed)
  const { data: mailbox } = await supabase.from('mailboxes').select('id, email_address, display_name').eq('id', 'b0000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001').maybeSingle();
  if (mailbox) {
    // Also delete related signatures for this mailbox
    const { data: sigs } = await supabase.from('signatures').select('id, mailbox_id').eq('mailbox_id', mailbox.id);
    if (sigs && sigs.length > 0) {
      const { error: sigErr } = await supabase.from('signatures').delete().eq('mailbox_id', mailbox.id);
      if (sigErr) throw sigErr;
      deleted.signatures = sigs;
    }
    const { error: mbErr } = await supabase.from('mailboxes').delete().eq('id', mailbox.id);
    if (mbErr) throw mbErr;
    deleted.mailbox = mailbox;
  }

  // 3. Delete demo staff user
  const { data: staffUser } = await supabase.from('staff_users').select('id, full_name, role, organization_id').eq('id', 'd0000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001').maybeSingle();
  if (staffUser) {
    const { error: staffErr } = await supabase.from('staff_users').delete().eq('id', staffUser.id);
    if (staffErr) throw staffErr;
    deleted.staffUser = staffUser;
  }

  // 4. Delete demo auth user
  const { data: authUser, error: authErr } = await supabase.auth.admin.getUserById('d0000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001');
  if (authErr) throw authErr;
  if (authUser.user) {
    const { error: delAuthErr } = await supabase.auth.admin.deleteUser(authUser.user.id);
    if (delAuthErr) throw delAuthErr;
    deleted.authUser = { id: authUser.user.id, email: authUser.user.email };
  }

  console.log('DELETED RECORDS SUMMARY');
  console.log(JSON.stringify(deleted, null, 2));
}

main().catch(err => {
  console.error('ERROR:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});
EOF

    13     const supabase = createClient(

    21       return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'mailbox_id, to[], subject required' }), {

    31       .single();
    32     if (mbError || !mailbox) throw new Error('Mailbox not found');

    37   msg: ParsedMessage,
    38 ): Promise<void> {
    39   const folderId = folderIdMap.get(msg.inboxFolderName) ?? null;
    40 
    41   // Extract message-id references for threading
    42   const referencedIds = [
    43     msg.headers['in-reply-to'],
    44     ...(msg.headers['references']?.split(/\s+/) ?? []),
    45   ].filter(Boolean);
    46 
    47   const threadId = await findOrCreateThread(
    48     supabase,
    49     mailboxId,
    50     msg.subject,
    51     msg.fromAddress,
    52     msg.toAddresses,
    53     referencedIds,
    54     folderId,
    55     msg.sentAt,
    56   );
    57 
    58   const messageId = await insertMessage(supabase, mailboxId, threadId, msg, folderId);
    59   if (!messageId) return; // Duplicate, skip
    60 
    61   // Store attachments
    62   if (msg.attachments.length) {
    63     await storeAttachments(supabase, mailboxId, messageId, msg.attachments);
    64   }
    65 
    66   // Spam detection (inbox messages only)
    67   if (!msg.isSpamFolder) {
    68     const headerAnalysis = analyzeSpamHeaders(msg.headers);
    69     if (headerAnalysis && headerAnalysis.is_spam) {
    70       // Layer 1: confirmed spam from headers
    71       await supabase.from('spam_flags').insert({
    72         message_id: messageId,
    73         source: 'spamassassin',
    74         confidence: headerAnalysis.confidence,
    75         reason: headerAnalysis.reason,
    76         user_action: 'pending',
    77       });
    78     } else if (needsAISecondPass(msg.headers)) {
    79       // Layer 2: AI second-pass for borderline / header-less
    80       const aiAnalysis = await analyzeSpamWithAI(msg.subject, msg.fromAddress, msg.bodyText);
    81       if (aiAnalysis.confidence >= 0.6) {
    82         await supabase.from('spam_flags').insert({
    83           message_id: messageId,
    84           source: 'ai_second_pass',
    85           confidence: aiAnalysis.confidence,
    86           reason: aiAnalysis.reason,
    87           user_action: 'pending',
    88         });
    89       }
    90     }
    91   }
    92 
    93   // Apply matching rules
    94   await applyRules(supabase, mailboxId, messageId, msg);
    95 
    96   // Update thread's last_message_at
    97   await supabase
    98     .from('threads')
    99     .update({ last_message_at: msg.sentAt.toISOString() })
   100     .eq('id', threadId)
   101     .lt('last_message_at', msg.sentAt.toISOString());
   102 }

   104 async function syncMailbox(mailboxRow: {
   105   id: string;
   106   email_address: string;
   107   imap_host: string;
   108   imap_port: number;
   109   credential_vault_ref: string;
   110 }): Promise<void> {
   111   const { id: mailboxId, email_address, imap_host, imap_port, credential_vault_ref } = mailboxRow;
   112   console.log(`[SYNC] Starting sync for ${email_address}`);
   113 
   114   let password: string;
   115   try {
   116     password = await getCredential(supabase, credential_vault_ref);
   117   } catch (err) {
   118     await supabase.from('mailboxes').update({
   119       sync_status: 'error',
   120       last_error: `Vault error: ${err}`,
   121     }).eq('id', mailboxId);
   122     return;
   123   }
   124 
   125   const client = new ImapClient({
   126     id: mailboxId,
   127     emailAddress: email_address,
   128     imapHost: imap_host,
   129     imapPort: imap_port,
   130     password,
   131   }, supabase);
   132 
   133   // High-priority folders get dedicated IMAP connections for near-real-time coverage
   134   const HIGH_PRIORITY_PATTERNS = ['inbox', 'sent', 'drafts', 'archive'];
   135   // Low-priority folders share a single polling connection at a slower interval
   136   const LOW_PRIORITY_PATTERNS = ['junk', 'trash', 'spam'];
   137   const LOW_PRIORITY_POLL_MS = 90_000; // 90 seconds
   138 
   139   // Track all per-folder clients so we can disconnect on error
   140   const folderClients: ImapClient[] = [];
   141 
   142   try {
   143     await client.connect();
   144     await supabase.from('mailboxes').update({ sync_status: 'syncing' }).eq('id', mailboxId);
   145 
   146     // Discover folders using the main client
   147     const folderNames = await client.listFolders();
   148     const folderIdMap = await syncFolders(supabase, mailboxId, folderNames);
   149 
   150     // Backfill all folders sequentially on the main connection
   151     for (const folderName of folderNames) {
   152       console.log(`[SYNC] Backfilling ${email_address}/${folderName}`);
   153       try {
   154         await client.backfill(folderName, BACKFILL_DAYS, async (msg) => {
   155           await processMessage(supabase, mailboxId, folderIdMap, msg);
   156         });
   157       } catch (err) {
   158         console.warn(`[SYNC] Backfill failed for ${email_address}/${folderName}:`, err);
   159       }
   160     }
   161 
   162     await supabase.from('mailboxes').update({
   163       sync_status: 'active',
   164       last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   165       last_error: null,
   166     }).eq('id', mailboxId);
   167 
   168     console.log(`[SYNC] Backfill complete for ${email_address}, entering multi-connection watch`);
   169 
   170     // Disconnect main client — dedicated per-folder clients take over
   171     await client.disconnect();
   172 
   173     // Categorise available folders
   174     const highPriorityFolders = folderNames.filter(name =>
   175       HIGH_PRIORITY_PATTERNS.some(p => name.toLowerCase().includes(p))
   176     );
   177     const lowPriorityFolders = folderNames.filter(name =>
   178       LOW_PRIORITY_PATTERNS.some(p => name.toLowerCase().includes(p))
   179     );
   180 
   181     const config: MailboxConfig = { id: mailboxId, emailAddress: email_address, imapHost: imap_host, imapPort: imap_port, password };
   182 
   183     // Spawn a dedicated IMAP connection per high-priority folder
   184     const highPriorityPromises = highPriorityFolders.map(async (folderName) => {
   185       const fc = new ImapClient(config, supabase);
   186       folderClients.push(fc);
   187       try {
   188         await fc.connect();
   189         console.log(`[SYNC] Dedicated watcher started for ${email_address}/${folderName}`);
   190         await fc.watchFolders([folderName], async (msg) => {
   191           await processMessage(supabase, mailboxId, folderIdMap, msg);
   192         }, POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
   193       } catch (err) {
   194         console.error(`[SYNC] Watcher error for ${email_address}/${folderName}:`, err);
   195         throw err; // propagate so the outer Promise.race can detect failure
   196       } finally {
   197         await fc.disconnect().catch(() => {});
   198       }
   199     });
   200 
   201     // Spawn one shared polling connection for low-priority folders
   202     let lowPriorityPromise: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
   203     if (lowPriorityFolders.length) {
   204       const lpc = new ImapClient(config, supabase);
   205       folderClients.push(lpc);
   206       lowPriorityPromise = (async () => {
   207         await lpc.connect();
   208         console.log(`[SYNC] Low-priority poller started for ${email_address}: ${lowPriorityFolders.join(', ')}`);
   209         await lpc.watchFolders(lowPriorityFolders, async (msg) => {
   210           await processMessage(supabase, mailboxId, folderIdMap, msg);
   211         }, LOW_PRIORITY_POLL_MS);
   212       })().catch(err => {
   213         console.error(`[SYNC] Low-priority poller error for ${email_address}:`, err);
   214         throw err;
   215       }).finally(async () => {
   216         await lpc.disconnect().catch(() => {});
   217       });
   218     }
   219 
   220     // If any folder connection drops, treat the whole mailbox sync as failed (triggers reconnect)
   221     await Promise.race([...highPriorityPromises, lowPriorityPromise]);
   222     throw new Error('A folder watcher exited unexpectedly');
   223 
   224   } catch (err) {
   225     console.error(`[SYNC] Error for ${email_address}:`, err);
   226     await supabase.from('mailboxes').update({
   227       sync_status: 'error',
   228       last_error: String(err),
   229     }).eq('id', mailboxId);
   230     // Clean up all folder clients
   231     await Promise.allSettled(folderClients.map(fc => fc.disconnect()));
   232     await client.disconnect().catch(() => {});
   233   }
   234 }

   236 /** Build an SmtpClient for a mailbox, fetching the password from Vault */
   237 interface SmtpMailbox {

   246 async function getSmtpClient(mailbox: SmtpMailbox): Promise<SmtpClient> {

   257 /** Resolve reply-to headers for threading from an original message */
   258 async function resolveReplyHeaders(replyToMessageId?: string | null): Promise<{ inReplyTo?: string; references?: string }> {
   259   if (!replyToMessageId) return {};
   260   const { data: origMsg } = await supabase
   261     .from('messages')
   262     .select('raw_headers')
   263     .eq('id', replyToMessageId)
   264     .maybeSingle();
   265   if (!origMsg?.raw_headers) return {};
   266   const headers = origMsg.raw_headers as Record<string, string>;
   267   const inReplyTo = headers['message-id'] ?? headers['Message-ID'];
   268   const references = [headers['references'], inReplyTo].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
   269   return { inReplyTo, references };
   270 }

   272 /** Insert a sent copy into messages table */
   273 async function recordSentMessage(opts: {
   274   mailbox_id: string;
   275   reply_to_message_id?: string | null;
   276   from_address: string;
   277   from_name?: string | null;
   278   to_addresses: string[];
   279   cc_addresses?: string[];
   280   bcc_addresses?: string[];
   281   subject: string;
   282   body_html: string;
   283   body_text: string;
   284   message_id: string;
   285 }): Promise<void> {
   286   let threadId: string | null = null;
   287 
   288   if (opts.reply_to_message_id) {
   289     const { data: existingThread } = await supabase
   290       .from('messages')
   291       .select('thread_id')
   292       .eq('id', opts.reply_to_message_id)
   293       .maybeSingle();
   294     threadId = existingThread?.thread_id ?? null;
   295   }
   296 
   297   if (!threadId) {
   298     // Create a thread for this outbound message
   299     const participants = [opts.from_address, ...opts.to_addresses, ...(opts.cc_addresses ?? []), ...(opts.bcc_addresses ?? [])];
   300     const { data: thread, error: threadErr } = await supabase
   301       .from('threads')
   302       .insert({
   303         mailbox_id: opts.mailbox_id,
   304         subject: opts.subject,
   305         participants: Array.from(new Set(participants)),
   306         folder_id: null,
   307         last_message_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   308         is_read: true,
   309         is_starred: false,
   310         labels: [],
   311       })
   312       .select('id')
   313       .single();
   314 
   315     if (threadErr) throw new Error(`Failed to create sent thread: ${threadErr.message}`);
   316     threadId = thread?.id ?? null;
   317   }
   318 
   319   const { error } = await supabase.from('messages').insert({
   320     thread_id: threadId,
   321     mailbox_id: opts.mailbox_id,
   322     from_address: opts.from_address,
   323     from_name: opts.from_name ?? null,
   324     to_addresses: opts.to_addresses,
   325     cc_addresses: opts.cc_addresses ?? [],
   326     bcc_addresses: opts.bcc_addresses ?? [],
   327     subject: opts.subject,
   328     body_html: opts.body_html,
   329     body_text: opts.body_text,
   330     sent_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   331     is_read: true,
   332     is_flagged: false,
   333     spam_status: 'clean',
   334     raw_headers: { 'message-id': opts.message_id },
   335   });
   336 
   337   if (error) {
   338     console.error('[SMTP] Failed to record sent message:', error);
   339     throw new Error(`Failed to record sent message: ${error.message}`);
   340   }
   341 }

   343 /** Send an outbound email with the email-safe pipeline and persist a sent copy */
   344 async function sendOutboundEmail(outbound: {
   345   id: string;
   346   mailbox_id: string;
   347   to_addresses: string[];
   348   cc_addresses: string[];
   349   bcc_addresses: string[];
   350   subject: string;
   351   body_html: string;
   352   reply_to_message_id?: string | null;
   353   attachments_json?: unknown;
   354 }, mailbox: {
   355   id: string;
   356   email_address: string;
   357   smtp_host: string;
   358   smtp_port: number;
   359   credential_vault_ref: string;
   360   display_name?: string | null;
   361 }, source: 'outbound' | 'scheduled'): Promise<void> {
   362   const smtp = await getSmtpClient(mailbox);
   363   const { inReplyTo, references } = await resolveReplyHeaders(outbound.reply_to_message_id);
   364 
   365   const attachments: Array<{ filename: string; content: Buffer; mimeType: string }> = [];
   366   const rawAttachments = (outbound.attachments_json ?? []) as Array<{ path: string; filename: string; mimeType?: string }>;
   367   for (const att of rawAttachments) {
   368     if (att?.path) {
   369       const { data: fileData, error: dlErr } = await supabase.storage.from('attachments').download(att.path);
   370       if (fileData && !dlErr) {
   371         const buffer = Buffer.from(await fileData.arrayBuffer());
   372         attachments.push({ filename: att.filename, content: buffer, mimeType: att.mimeType ?? 'application/octet-stream' });
   373       }
   374     }
   375   }
   376 
   377   const result = await smtp.sendMail({
   378     to: outbound.to_addresses,
   379     cc: outbound.cc_addresses,
   380     bcc: outbound.bcc_addresses,
   381     subject: outbound.subject,
   382     htmlBody: outbound.body_html,
   383     inReplyTo,
   384     references,
   385     attachments,
   386   });
   387 
   388   await recordSentMessage({
   389     mailbox_id: outbound.mailbox_id,
   390     reply_to_message_id: outbound.reply_to_message_id,
   391     from_address: mailbox.email_address,
   392     from_name: mailbox.display_name,
   393     to_addresses: outbound.to_addresses,
   394     cc_addresses: outbound.cc_addresses,
   395     bcc_addresses: outbound.bcc_addresses,
   396     subject: outbound.subject,
   397     body_html: result.html,
   398     body_text: result.text,
   399     message_id: result.messageId,
   400   });
   401 
   402   console.log(`[SMTP] ${source} message ${outbound.id} sent as ${result.messageId}`);
   403 }

   405 /** Process scheduled messages due to be sent */
   406 async function processScheduledMessages(): Promise<void> {
   407   const { data: due } = await supabase
   408     .from('scheduled_messages')
   409     .select('*, mailboxes(email_address, smtp_host, smtp_port, credential_vault_ref, display_name)')
   410     .eq('status', 'pending')
   411     .lte('send_at', new Date().toISOString())
   412     .limit(10);
   413 
   414   if (!due?.length) return;
   415 
   416   for (const scheduled of due) {
   417     try {
   418       const mailbox = (scheduled as Record<string, unknown>).mailboxes as {
   419         email_address: string;
   420         smtp_host: string;
   421         smtp_port: number;
   422         credential_vault_ref: string;
   423         display_name: string;
   424       };
   425 
   426       await sendOutboundEmail(
   427         {
   428           id: scheduled.id as string,
   429           mailbox_id: scheduled.mailbox_id as string,
   430           to_addresses: (scheduled.to_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   431           cc_addresses: (scheduled.cc_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   432           bcc_addresses: (scheduled.bcc_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   433           subject: scheduled.subject as string,
   434           body_html: scheduled.body_html as string,
   435           reply_to_message_id: (scheduled.reply_to_message_id ?? null) as string | null,
   436           attachments_json: scheduled.attachments_json ?? [],
   437         },
   438         { ...mailbox, id: scheduled.mailbox_id as string },
   439         'scheduled',
   440       );
   441 
   442       await supabase.from('scheduled_messages')
   443         .update({ status: 'sent', sent_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   444         .eq('id', scheduled.id);
   445       console.log(`[SCHEDULED] Sent scheduled message ${scheduled.id}`);
   446     } catch (err) {
   447       console.error(`[SCHEDULED] Failed to send ${scheduled.id}:`, err);
   448       await supabase.from('scheduled_messages')
   449         .update({ status: 'failed' })
   450         .eq('id', scheduled.id);
   451     }
   452   }
   453 }

   455 /** Process queued outbound messages */
   456 async function processOutboundMessages(): Promise<void> {
   457   const { data: pending, error } = await supabase
   458     .from('outbound_messages')
   459     .select('*, mailboxes(email_address, smtp_host, smtp_port, credential_vault_ref, display_name)')
   460     .eq('status', 'pending')
   461     .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
   462     .limit(10);
   463 
   464   if (error) {
   465     console.error('[OUTBOUND] Failed to fetch queue:', error);
   466     return;
   467   }
   468   if (!pending?.length) return;
   469 
   470   for (const msg of pending) {
   471     const outbound = msg as Record<string, unknown>;
   472     const id = outbound.id as string;
   473 
   474     // Mark as sending to avoid duplicate picks from concurrent workers
   475     const { error: lockErr } = await supabase
   476       .from('outbound_messages')
   477       .update({ status: 'sending', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   478       .eq('id', id)
   479       .eq('status', 'pending');
   480     if (lockErr) continue;
   481 
   482     const mailbox = outbound.mailboxes as {
   483       email_address: string;
   484       smtp_host: string;
   485       smtp_port: number;
   486       credential_vault_ref: string;
   487       display_name: string;
   488     };
   489 
   490     try {
   491       await sendOutboundEmail(
   492         {
   493           id,
   494           mailbox_id: outbound.mailbox_id as string,
   495           to_addresses: (outbound.to_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   496           cc_addresses: (outbound.cc_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   497           bcc_addresses: (outbound.bcc_addresses ?? []) as string[],
   498           subject: outbound.subject as string,
   499           body_html: outbound.body_html as string,
   500           reply_to_message_id: (outbound.reply_to_message_id ?? null) as string | null,
   501           attachments_json: outbound.attachments_json ?? [],
   502         },
   503         { ...mailbox, id: outbound.mailbox_id as string },
   504         'outbound',
   505       );
   506 
   507       await supabase.from('outbound_messages')
   508         .update({ status: 'sent', sent_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   509         .eq('id', id);
   510     } catch (err) {
   511       console.error(`[OUTBOUND] Failed to send ${id}:`, err);
   512       await supabase.from('outbound_messages')
   513         .update({ status: 'failed', error: String(err), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
   514         .eq('id', id);
   515     }
   516   }
   517 }

   519 /** Track which mailbox IDs are already being synced to avoid duplicate connections */
   520 const activeSyncIds = new Set<string>();
   521 
   522 async function runMailboxLoop(mb: {
   523   id: string; email_address: string; imap_host: string;
   524   imap_port: number; credential_vault_ref: string;
   525 }, retryDelayMs = 30000): Promise<void> {
   526   try {
   527     await syncMailbox(mb);
   528     // Normal completion (shouldn't happen while watchFolder is alive)
   529     console.log(`[SYNC] Sync ended normally for ${mb.email_address}`);
   530   } catch (err) {
   531     console.error(`[SYNC] Fatal error for ${mb.email_address}:`, err);
   532   } finally {
   533     activeSyncIds.delete(mb.id);
   534   }
   535 
   536   // Auto-reconnect with backoff
   537   console.log(`[SYNC] Reconnecting ${mb.email_address} in ${retryDelayMs / 1000}s`);
   538   await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, retryDelayMs));
   539   await startMailboxSync(mb, Math.min(retryDelayMs * 2, 300000)); // cap at 5 min
   540 }
   541 
   542 function startMailboxSync(mb: {
   543   id: string; email_address: string; imap_host: string;
   544   imap_port: number; credential_vault_ref: string;
   545 }, retryDelayMs = 30000): void {
   546   if (activeSyncIds.has(mb.id)) {
   547     console.log(`[SYNC] Already syncing ${mb.email_address}, skipping`);
   548     return;
   549   }
   550   activeSyncIds.add(mb.id);
   551   // Run the sync loop in the background so the caller isn't blocked
   552   runMailboxLoop(mb, retryDelayMs).catch(err => {
   553     console.error(`[SYNC] Unhandled loop error for ${mb.email_address}:`, err);
   554     activeSyncIds.delete(mb.id);
   555   });
   556 }

    28   constructor(config: SmtpConfig) {

    51 
    52   async sendMail(opts: SendMailOptions): Promise<{ messageId: string; html: string; text: string }> {
    53     const safe = emailSafePipeline(opts.htmlBody);
    54     const info = await this.transporter.sendMail({
    55       from: `"${this.config.displayName ?? ''}" <${this.config.emailAddress}>`,
    56       to: opts.to.join(', '),
    57       cc: opts.cc?.join(', '),
    58       bcc: opts.bcc?.join(', '),
    59       subject: opts.subject,
    60       html: safe.html,
    61       text: safe.text,
    62       inReplyTo: opts.inReplyTo,
    63       references: opts.references,
    64       attachments: opts.attachments?.map(a => ({
    65         filename: a.filename,
    66         content: a.content,
    67         contentType: a.mimeType,
    68       })),
    69     });
    70     return { messageId: info.messageId, html: safe.html, text: safe.text };
    71   }
    72 }

   593   process.on('uncaughtException', err => console.error('[FATAL] Uncaught exception:', err));
   594   process.on('unhandledRejection', err => console.error('[FATAL] Unhandled rejection:', err));

   597   writeHeartbeat();
   598   setInterval(writeHeartbeat, 10_000);

   601   const { data: mailboxes, error } = await supabase
   602     .from('mailboxes')
   603     .select('id, email_address, imap_host, imap_port, credential_vault_ref')
   604     .not('credential_vault_ref', 'is', null)
   605     .in('sync_status', ['pending', 'active', 'error', 'syncing']);

   613     console.log('[INIT] No credentialed mailboxes yet — waiting for credentials to be added...');

   615     for (const mb of mailboxes) {
   616       await startMailboxSync(mb);
   617       await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000)); // stagger connections
   618     }

   622   setInterval(() => {
   623     processOutboundMessages().catch(err => console.error('[OUTBOUND] Error:', err));

<action>
**FileEditAction**
Path: [/workspace/app-cr8gr9rfgsn5/sync-service/src/index.ts]
Thought: Found it. **The `setInterval` for outbound polling only registers AFTER the `for...of` mailbox startup loop completes.** With 34 mailboxes each staggered 2 seconds, that loop takes **68+ seconds minimum** before intervals even start. Since the service is still in the startup loop, no outbound polling has fired yet.

The fix: run the first outbound poll immediately at startup, and also fire it before the stagger loop begins.
Command: str_replace
Old String: ```
  // Outbound send queue processor
  setInterval(() => {
    processOutboundMessages().catch(err => console.error('[OUTBOUND] Error:', err));
  }, OUTBOUND_POLL_INTERVAL);

  // Scheduled messages checker
  setInterval(() => {
    processScheduledMessages().catch(err => console.error('[SCHEDULED] Error:', err));
  }, SCHEDULED_CHECK_INTERVAL);
```
New String: ```
  // Outbound send queue processor — run immediately then every 10 s
  processOutboundMessages().catch(err => console.error('[OUTBOUND] Error:', err));
  setInterval(() => {
    processOutboundMessages().catch(err => console.error('[OUTBOUND] Error:', err));
  }, OUTBOUND_POLL_INTERVAL);

  // Scheduled messages checker — run immediately then every 60 s
  processScheduledMessages().catch(err => console.error('[SCHEDULED] Error:', err));
  setInterval(() => {
    processScheduledMessages().catch(err => console.error('[SCHEDULED] Error:', err));
  }, SCHEDULED_CHECK_INTERVAL);
```

**CmdRunAction (source=EventSource.AGENT, is_input=False)**

cat > /workspace/app-cr8gr9rfgsn5/tasks/list-folders.cjs << 'EOF'
const { createClient } = require('@supabase/supabase-js');
const sb = createClient(
  'https://hgzyypyqawcppivnghpr.supabase.co',
  'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6Imhnenl5cHlxYXdjcHBpdm5naHByIiwicm9sZSI6InNlcnZpY2Vfcm9sZSIsImlhdCI6MTc4MzA0NDE3MCwiZXhwIjoyMDk4NjIwMTcwfQ.U8lrNfsqklbAzYa0jmjo3wWPpWmFazWDeRY47kGhYBs'
);
async function main() {
  const { data, error } = await sb
    .from('folders')
    .select('mailbox_id, name, sync_enabled, last_synced_at')
    .order('mailbox_id')
    .limit(100);
  if (error) console.error(error);
  else {
    console.log(`Total folders: ${data.length}`);
    const grouped = data.reduce((acc, f) => {
      if (!acc[f.mailbox_id]) acc[f.mailbox_id] = [];
      acc[f.mailbox_id].push(f);
      return acc;
    }, {});
    Object.entries(grouped).forEach(([id, folders]) => {
      console.log(`\nMailbox ${id.slice(0, 8)}: ${folders.map(f => f.name).join(', ')}`);
    });

   214         throw err;
   215       }).finally(async () => {
   216         await lpc.disconnect().catch(() => {});
   217       });
   218     }
   219

   221     await Promise.race([...highPriorityPromises, lowPriorityPromise]);

    14 function parseAddresses(raw: string | undefined): string[] {
    15   if (!raw) return [];
    16   return raw.split(',').map(a => {
    17     const m = a.match(/<([^>]+)>/);
    18     return (m ? m[1] : a).trim();
    19   }).filter(Boolean);

    23 function flattenHeaders(headers: Headers | Record<string, string>): Record<string, string> {
    24   const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    25   if (headers instanceof Headers) {
    26     headers.forEach((v, k) => { out[k.toLowerCase()] = v; });
    27   } else {
    28     for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(headers)) {
    29       out[k.toLowerCase()] = String(v);
    30     }
    31   }
    32   return out;

    28 /** Ensure mailbox_folders rows exist for a given IMAP folder list */
    29 export async function syncFolders(
    30   supabase: SupabaseClient,
    31   mailboxId: string,
    32   imapFolderNames: string[],
    33 ): Promise<Map<string, string>> {
    34   const folderIdMap = new Map<string, string>(); // imap_name -> id
    35 
    36   for (const name of imapFolderNames) {
    37     const normalized = normalizeFolder(name);
    38     const displayName = getFolderDisplayName(name);
    39 
    40     const { data: existing } = await supabase
    41       .from('mailbox_folders')
    42       .select('id')
    43       .eq('mailbox_id', mailboxId)
    44       .eq('imap_folder_name', name)
    45       .maybeSingle();
    46 
    47     if (existing) {
    48       folderIdMap.set(name, existing.id);
    49     } else {
    50       const { data: inserted } = await supabase
    51         .from('mailbox_folders')
    52         .insert({ mailbox_id: mailboxId, imap_folder_name: name, normalized_type: normalized, display_name: displayName })
    53         .select('id')
    54         .single();
    55       if (inserted) folderIdMap.set(name, inserted.id);
    56     }
    57   }
    58 
    59   return folderIdMap;
    60 }
    61 
    62 /** Find or create a thread for a message, using In-Reply-To/References threading */
    63 export async function findOrCreateThread(
    64   supabase: SupabaseClient,
    65   mailboxId: string,
    66   subject: string,
    67   fromAddress: string,
    68   toAddresses: string[],
    69   referencedMessageIds: string[],
    70   folderId: string | null,
    71   sentAt: Date,
    72 ): Promise<string> {
    73   // Try to find existing thread via message-id references
    74   for (const msgId of referencedMessageIds) {
    75     const { data: msg } = await supabase
    76       .from('messages')
    77       .select('thread_id')
    78       .contains('raw_headers', { 'message-id': msgId })
    79       .maybeSingle();
    80     if (msg?.thread_id) return msg.thread_id;

   135   private parseMessage(msg: any, folderName: string): ParsedMessage {
   136     const envelope = msg.envelope;
   137     const headers = flattenHeaders(msg.headers as unknown as Record<string, string>);
   138     const parsed: ParsedMessage = {
   139       imapUid: msg.uid,
   140       imapUidvalidity: 0,
   141       subject: envelope?.subject ?? '(no subject)',
   142       fromAddress: envelope?.from?.[0]?.address ?? '',
   143       fromName: envelope?.from?.[0]?.name ?? envelope?.from?.[0]?.address ?? '',
   144       toAddresses: envelope?.to?.map((a: any) => a.address ?? '').filter(Boolean) ?? [],
   145       ccAddresses: envelope?.cc?.map((a: any) => a.address ?? '').filter(Boolean) ?? [],
   146       bccAddresses: envelope?.bcc?.map((a: any) => a.address ?? '').filter(Boolean) ?? [],
   147       bodyHtml: '',
   148       bodyText: '',
   149       sentAt: envelope?.date ?? new Date(),
   150       headers,
   151       attachments: [],
   152       inboxFolderName: folderName,
   153       isRead: msg.flags?.has('\\Seen') ?? false,
   154       isSpamFolder: ['junk', 'spam'].includes(folderName.toLowerCase()),
   155     };
   156     const htmlPart = msg.bodyParts?.get('TEXT');
   157     if (htmlPart) {
   158       const text = Buffer.from(htmlPart as Uint8Array).toString('utf8');
   159       if (text.includes('<')) {
   160         parsed.bodyHtml = text;
   161         parsed.bodyText = text.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
   162       } else {
   163         parsed.bodyText = text;
   164       }
   165     }
   166     return parsed;
   167   }
   168 
   169   /** Poll multiple folders sequentially on one connection. Returns only on error. */
   170   async watchFolders(
   171     folderNames: string[],
   172     onMessage: (msg: ParsedMessage) => Promise<void>,
   173     pollIntervalMs = 30000,
   174   ): Promise<void> {
   175     if (!folderNames.length) throw new Error('watchFolders: no folder names provided');
   176 
   177     // Seed last seen UID + UIDVALIDITY per folder (skip empty folders)
   178     const folderState = new Map<string, { lastSeenUid: number; uidValidity: number; isEmpty: boolean }>();
   179     for (const folderName of folderNames) {
   180       let isEmpty = false;
   181       try {
   182         const status = await this.client.status(folderName, { messages: true, uidNext: true, uidValidity: true });
   183         const msgCount = Number(status.messages ?? 0);
   184         if (msgCount === 0) {
   185           folderState.set(folderName, { lastSeenUid: 0, uidValidity: Number(status.uidValidity ?? 0), isEmpty: true });
   186           console.log(`[IMAP] Folder ${folderName} is empty, skipping UID seed`);
   187           continue;
   188         }
   189       } catch (err) {
   190         console.warn(`[IMAP] Could not get status for ${folderName}:`, err);
   191       }
   192 
   193       try {
   194         const lock = await this.client.getMailboxLock(folderName);
   195         try {
   196           const mailboxObj = this.client.mailbox;
   197           const uidValidity = mailboxObj ? Number(mailboxObj.uidValidity) : 0;
   198           let lastSeenUid = 0;
   199           for await (const msg of this.client.fetch('1:*', { uid: true })) {
   200             if (msg.uid && msg.uid > lastSeenUid) lastSeenUid = msg.uid;
   201           }
   202           folderState.set(folderName, { lastSeenUid, uidValidity, isEmpty: false });
   203         } finally {
   204           lock.release();
   205         }
   206       } catch (err) {
   207         console.warn(`[IMAP] Could not seed state for ${folderName}:`, err);
   208         folderState.set(folderName, { lastSeenUid: 0, uidValidity: 0, isEmpty: true });
   209       }
   210     }
   211 
   212     console.log(`[IMAP] Polling ${folderNames.length} folder(s) every ${pollIntervalMs / 1000}s: ${folderNames.join(', ')}`);
   213 
   214     let connectionAlive = true;
   215     this.client.on('error', (err: Error) => {
   216       console.error('[IMAP] Connection error:', err);
   217       connectionAlive = false;
   218     });
   219     this.client.on('close', () => {
   220       console.error('[IMAP] Connection closed');

New String: ```
    // Disconnect main client — dedicated per-folder clients take over
    await client.disconnect();

    // Categorise available folders: high-priority get dedicated connections,
    // EVERYTHING else is batched on a shared "all other folders" poller so
    // custom folders (e.g. Archive, Templates, company folders) are also synced.
    const highPrioritySet = new Set<string>();
    const otherFolders: string[] = [];
    for (const name of folderNames) {
      if (HIGH_PRIORITY_PATTERNS.some(p => name.toLowerCase().includes(p))) {
        highPrioritySet.add(name);
      } else {
        otherFolders.push(name);
      }
    }

    const config: MailboxConfig = { id: mailboxId, emailAddress: email_address, imapHost: imap_host, imapPort: imap_port, password };

    // Spawn a dedicated IMAP connection per high-priority folder
    const highPriorityPromises = Array.from(highPrioritySet).map(async (folderName) => {
      const fc = new ImapClient(config, supabase);
      folderClients.push(fc);
      try {
        await fc.connect();
        console.log(`[SYNC] Dedicated watcher started for ${email_address}/${folderName}`);
        await fc.watchFolders([folderName], async (msg) => {
          await processMessage(supabase, mailboxId, folderIdMap, msg);
        }, POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`[SYNC] Watcher error for ${email_address}/${folderName}:`, err);
        throw err; // propagate so the outer Promise.race can detect failure
      } finally {
        await fc.disconnect().catch(() => {});
      }
    });

    // Spawn one shared polling connection for ALL non-high-priority folders
    let otherFoldersPromise: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
    if (otherFolders.length) {
      const ofc = new ImapClient(config, supabase);
      folderClients.push(ofc);
      otherFoldersPromise = (async () => {
        await ofc.connect();
        console.log(`[SYNC] All-other-folders poller started for ${email_address}: ${otherFolders.join(', ')}`);
        await ofc.watchFolders(otherFolders, async (msg) => {
          await processMessage(supabase, mailboxId, folderIdMap, msg);
        }, LOW_PRIORITY_POLL_MS);
      })().catch(err => {
        console.error(`[SYNC] All-other-folders poller error for ${email_address}:`, err);
        throw err;
      }).finally(async () => {
        await ofc.disconnect().catch(() => {});
      });
    }

    // If any folder connection drops, treat the whole mailbox sync as failed (triggers reconnect)
    await Promise.race([...highPriorityPromises, otherFoldersPromise]);
```
